# `Assembly.lean` implementation audit by Chapter 1 diagram node
## Authority and update rule
`to_formalize/original_erdos_64_proof.tex` is the sole authority for mathematical statements, hypotheses, alternatives, order, and terminal behavior. Live Lean declaration types, bodies, exact key lists, call sites, build results, and sealed reports are the authority for what the implementation does.
The two tables below are the sole authority for current implementation status and the next unresolved work. Whenever a fact changes, update its paper-label row and every corresponding diagram-node row in the same change. Do not add progress logs, dated snapshots, executive findings, or narrative gap sections. Stable rubric prose may change only when a column's meaning changes.
## Status rubric
A kernel-check pass establishes Lean type correctness only. It does not establish manuscript fidelity, residual locality, correct ledger registration, reachability, absence of unwanted axioms, or correct final wiring; those are evaluated separately.
“Local” and “Residual-local proof?” require a proposition about the literal active minimal-counterexample residual, derived from `inputs.current` and exact predecessor facts. “Reads ledger” and “Writes ledger” require semantic-key reads and publication through `FactManifest.Produces` or one arm of `Decision`. “Wired” and “Correctly wired?” require the final endpoint to traverse the exact predecessor and branch. “Legal” and “Custom illegal carrier?” enforce the proof-specific declaration boundary and the canonical `ExactLedger`/sealed Strategy APIs.
Status legend: ✅ verified from the current type, body, and build; ⚠ concrete partial implementation or unresolved defect; ❌ absent or verified failure; N/A only where the column does not apply. Comments and docstrings are search locators, never implementation evidence.
